\int_{Z_{{\mathbf{GSp}}_{6}}({\bf A})\backslash[\mathbf{GL}_{2}\boxtimes{\mathbf{GSp}}_{4}]}E_{P_{(1,4)}}(\iota(g_{1},g_{2}),s,\phi_{s}^{\prime})\Psi^{w}(g_{1})\varphi(g_{2})\,dg_{1}\,dg_{2}

is identically zero.

In what follows, we assume that
\omega_{\pi}\omega_{\sigma}\neq 1
and
(\omega_{\pi}\omega_{\sigma})^{2}=1
and give a period condition for the vanishing of the residue at
s=1
of the partial
L
-function
L^{S}(s,\pi\otimes\sigma,\wedge^{2}\otimes\mathrm{std}_{2})
. We follow a similar strategy to that of §
6
: we first use Corollary
5.3
and then apply the relevant Siegel–Weil formulae.

8.1.
An application of the Siegel-Weil formula

For simplicity, denote by
\chi
the quadratic Hecke character
\omega_{\pi}\omega_{\sigma}
on
F^{\times}\backslash{\bf A}^{\times}
. Associated to
\chi
, there is a unique quadratic field extension

F_{\chi}/F
. When endowed with the quadratic form
N_{F_{\chi}/F}
, it defines a quadratic space over
F
of dimension
2
.

Let
\tilde{I}_{P_{(3,0)}}(s,\chi)
be the restriction to
{\mathbf{Sp}}_{6}({\bf A})
of the induction representation
I_{P_{(3,0)}}(s,\chi)
. Let
V_{\chi}
be any quadratic space over
F
of dimension
2
with character
\chi
. By
[
36
]
, we have a map

\lambda_{V_{\chi}}:\mathcal{S}(V_{\chi}({\bf A})^{3})\to\tilde{I}_{P_{(3,0)}}(0,\chi).

Its image is denoted by
\tilde{\Pi}(V_{\chi})
.

For the next Theorem, let
M^{*}(1,\chi):\tilde{I}_{P_{(3,0)}}(1,\chi)\to\tilde{I}_{P_{(3,0)}}(0,\chi)
be the (normalized) intertwining operator defined as in
[
36
, p. 4]
.

Theorem 8.3
.

Let
\phi_{s}\in I_{P_{(3,0)}}\left(s,\chi\right)
be a section such that
M^{*}(1,\chi){\phi_{1}}|_{{\mathbf{Sp}}_{6}({\bf A})}\in\tilde{\Pi}(V_{\chi})
. For any
g\in{\mathbf{Sp}}_{6}({\bf A})
, we have

\mathrm{Res}_{s=1}E_{6}\left(g,s,\phi_{s}\right)=c_{0}\int_{[\mathbf{O}_{{V_{\chi}}}]}\theta(g,h,\varphi_{V_{\chi}})dh,

where
\varphi_{V_{\chi}}\in\mathcal{S}(V_{\chi}({\bf A})^{3})
is so that
\lambda_{V_{\chi}}(\varphi_{V_{\chi}})=M^{*}(1,\chi){\phi_{1}}|_{{\mathbf{Sp}}_{6}({\bf A})}
and
c_{0}\in{\bf C}^{\times}
.

Proof.

This is
[
36
, Corollary 6.3]
for the quadratic space
V_{\chi}\oplus\mathbb{H}^{2}
, which has complementary space
V_{\chi}
in the notation of
loc.cit.
.
∎

Remark 8.4
.

In contrast to §
6
, we cannot extend the formula of Theorem
8.3
to the case of similitude groups. The reason being that the translates of
\lambda_{V_{\chi}}(\varphi_{V_{\chi}})
by elements in
{\mathbf{GSp}}_{6}^{+}({\bf A})
might not come from any
\mathcal{S}(V({\bf A})^{3})
, where
V
is an anisotropic quadratic space of dimension
2
over
F
of character
\chi
.

Proposition 8.5
.

If
{\rm Res}_{s=1}Z(s,\varphi,f^{\Psi}_{\phi_{s}})\neq 0
, then there exists
\varphi_{V_{\chi}}\in\mathcal{S}(V_{\chi}({\bf A})^{3})
such that

\int_{[\mathbf{O}_{V_{\chi}}]}\int_{[\mathbf{PSL}_{2}\times\mathbf{PSp}_{4}]}\theta(\iota(g_{1},g_{2}),h,\varphi_{V_{\chi}})\Psi^{w}(g_{1})\varphi(g_{2})dg_{1}dg_{2}\neq 0.

Proof.

If
{\rm Res}_{s=1}Z(s,\varphi,f^{\Psi}_{\phi_{s}})\neq 0
, then

{\rm Res}_{s=1}\int_{[\mathbf{PSp}_{4}]}E^{*}_{P_{(1,2)}}(g,s,f^{\Psi}_{\phi_{s}})\varphi(g)dg\neq 0.

Then, by Garrett’s pullback formula and the Siegel-Weil formula of Theorem
8.3
, the non-vanishing of
{\rm Res}_{s=1}Z(s,\varphi,f^{\Psi}_{\phi_{s}})\neq 0
implies that

\sum_{V_{\chi}}\int_{[\mathbf{O}_{V_{\chi}}]}\int_{[\mathbf{PSL}_{2}\times\mathbf{PSp}_{4}]}\theta(\iota(g_{1},g_{2}),h,\varphi_{V_{\chi}})\Psi^{w}(g_{1})\varphi(g_{2})dg_{1}dg_{2}\neq 0,

where, we have used that, by
[
36
, Theorem 4.9]
,

M^{*}(1,\chi)\phi_{1}\mapsto\mathrm{Res}_{s=1}E^{*}_{P_{(3,0)}}(\iota(g_{1},g_{2}),s,M^{*}(s,\chi)\phi_{s})

factors through
\bigoplus_{V_{\chi}}\tilde{\Pi}(V_{\chi})
, and we have denoted
\varphi_{V_{\chi}}=\mathrm{pr}_{\tilde{\Pi}(V)}(M^{*}(1,\chi)\phi_{1})
.
∎

Applying Theorem
3.5
and Proposition
8.5
, we get the following.

Corollary 8.6
.

Let
\phi_{s}\in I_{P_{(3,0)}}\left(s,\chi\right)
be a section such that
M^{*}(1,\chi){\phi_{1}}|_{{\mathbf{Sp}}_{6}({\bf A})}\in\tilde{\Pi}(V_{\chi})
. Suppose that there exist data
W_{\pi_{v}}
and
W_{\sigma_{v},f_{\phi_{1/2,v}}^{\Psi_{v}}}
such that
\prod_{v\in S}Z\left(1,W_{\pi_{v}},W_{\sigma_{v},f_{\phi_{1/2,v}}^{\Psi_{v}}}\right)\neq 0,
then

{\rm Res}_{s=1}L^{S}(s,\pi\otimes\sigma,\wedge^{2}\otimes{\rm std}_{2})\neq 0\Longrightarrow\int_{[\mathbf{O}_{V_{\chi}}]}\int_{[\mathbf{PSL}_{2}\times\mathbf{PSp}_{4}]}\theta(\iota(g_{1},g_{2}),h,\varphi_{V_{\chi}})\Psi^{w}(g_{1})\varphi(g_{2})dg_{1}dg_{2}\neq 0.
